Gradient descent procedure:
```
optimize_mirror(p, time_budget):
   p = p.copy(); best=p; best_score=score_fast(p); n=len(p)
   eta=1e-3; tau_factor=20
   while time left:
       c = conv_full(p)
       maxc = c.max(); if maxc<=0 return
       tau = tau_factor / maxc
       vals = tau*(c - maxc)
       # clip vals to [-700,700]
       w = exp(vals); w/=w.sum()
       q = gradient_vec(p,w) # 2 included? h=2q maybe
       h = 2*q
       # normalize gradient to unit RMS for step
       h = h - h.mean()? In mirror on simplex, subtract mean not necessary due normalization after, but can center to reduce scale drift: h = h - h.mean()
       scale = eta * (np.sqrt(np.mean(h*h))+1e-12)
       logp = np.log(p + 1e-300) - scale * h
       p_new = np.exp(logp - np.max(logp)); p_new/=p_new.sum()
       s_new=score_fast(p_new)
       if s_new < best_score: best=p_new; best_score=s_new; eta*=1.1?; tau_factor maybe increase
       else: eta*=0.5; if eta tiny...
```

2. next_pow2:

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```

3. central_sequence(n):
```
def _central_sequence(n):
    c=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if n>1:
       val=1.0
       for i in range(1,n):
          val *= (2*i -1)/(2*i)
          c[i]=val
    return c
```
For n large, val may underflow? n=4096, c ~0.017, fine.

4. convolution_fft(a):
```
def _convolution_fft(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    need=2*n-1
    nfft=_next_pow2(need)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
    ac=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fa),nfft)
    ac=ac[:need]
    # remove tiny neg
    ac=np.where(ac<0,0.0,ac)
    return ac
```
For gradient q, need convolve A and B with different lengths; use full fft.
```
def _convolve_fft_general(x,y):
   need=len(x)+len(y)-1
   nfft=_next_pow2(need)
   X=np.fft.fft(x,nfft); Y=np.fft.fft(y,nfft)
   res=np.fft.ifft(X*Y).real[:need]
   return res
```
Could use rfft but general okay.

5. score_fast(a):
```
def _score_fast(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return inf
   s=float(np.sum(a))
   if s<=0 or not finite: return inf
   c=_convolution_fft(a)
   mc=float(np.max(c))
   if mc<=0 or not finite: return inf
   return float(2.0*n*mc/(s*s))
```
Since evaluate_sequence clamps negatives and caps, our p positive no issue.

6. gradient q:
```
def _gradient_vector(p,w):
   n=len(p)
   A=np.zeros(n); # offset index i -> n-1-j? Let's set A[n-1-j]=p[j]
   A[:] = p[::-1] ? If j=0 -> index n-1 =p0; j=n-1 -> index0=p[n-1]. So A = p[::-1] length n.
   B=w
   conv=_convolve_fft_general(A,B) # length 3n-3
   q=conv[(n-1):(2*n-1)] # indices n-1 .. n-1+n-2 inclusive length n-1? Wait need length n: 0 to n-1. Conv index n-1+i for i=0..n-1 => n-1 .. 2n-2 inclusive length n. Slice [n-1:2*n-1]. But conv length 3n-3, max index 3n-4. 2n-2 <=3n-4 for n>=2.
   return 2.0*q
```
Test for n=1? Convolution length1; skip optimize n<8. For n=1, A len1, B len1, conv len1, slice [0:1] works. But no need.

Let's verify with small n. p=[p0,p1], w=[w0,w1,w2]. q0 should ∑_{j}p_j w_{0+j}=p0 w0 + p1 w1. A=p[::-1]=[p1,p0]. B=[w0,w1,w2]. Conv: index n-1=1: A0B1 + A1B0 = p1 w1 + p0 w0. OK. q1 index2: A0B2 + A1B1 = p1 w2 + p0 w1. Desired q1=∑ p_j w_{1+j}=p0 w1 + p1 w2. OK.

7. smooth_weights(c, tau_factor):
```
def _smooth_weights(c, tau_factor=20.0):
   maxc=c.max()
   if maxc<=0: return uniform
   tau=tau_factor/maxc
   vals=tau*(c-maxc)
   vals=np.clip(vals,-700,700)
   # subtract max (0) already; exp <=1
   ex=np.exp(vals)
   s=ex.sum()
   if s<=0: uniform
   return ex/s
```
Mean? w sum1. Good.

8. mirror_update(p,w,eta):
```
def _mirror_update(p,w,eta):
   h=_gradient_vector(p,w)
   # center to preserve scale? 
   h = h - np.mean(h)
   norm = np.sqrt(np.mean(h*h)) + 1e-300
   h = h / norm
   logp = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)) - eta*h
   logp = logp - np.max(logp)
   pnew=np.exp(logp)
   pnew = pnew/np.sum(pnew)
   pnew = np.maximum(pnew,0.0)
   pnew = pnew/np.sum(pnew)
   return pnew
```
eta step scale. Since h normalized RMS1, eta ~0.1-1.0 maybe. We'll adapt.

But score gradient h is not normalized; after centering and norm, step size in log variables. Use eta starting 0.5. If score improves, increase eta; else halve.

Adaptive:
```
def _optimize_mirror(p, deadline, max_age=time?):
   p=p.copy(); best=p; best_score=_score_fast(p)
   eta=0.5
   tau_factor=20
   stall=0
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
      c=_convolution_fft(p); maxc=c.max()
      if maxc<=0: break
      w=_smooth_weights(c,tau_factor)
      p2=_mirror_update(p,w,eta)
      s2=_score_fast(p2)
      if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score*0.999999:
          best=p2; best_score=s2; p=p2; eta=min(3.0, eta*1.2); stall=0; print
      else:
          eta*=0.5
          if eta<1e-4:
              # random kick
              p = _random_perturb(p,rng) maybe
              eta=0.2
              # if still fail tau_factor vary
      # periodically increase tau_factor to sharpen
      tau_factor = min(100, tau_factor*1.05) ???
   return best,best_score
```

Local perturbation:
```
def _random_perturb(p,rng):
   n=len(p)
   q=p.copy()
   # multiplicative noise
   noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.05,n))
   q=q*noise
   # maybe shift mass
   q/=q.sum()
   return np.maximum(q,1e-12); q/=q.sum()
```

Let's design robust:
```
def _run_mirror(p, target_deadline, tag):
    p=p.copy(); n=len(p)
    best=p; best_score=_score_fast(p)
    tau_factor=10.0
    eta=0.1
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<target_deadline:
       c=_convolution_fft(p); maxc=c.max(); s=p.sum()
       cur_score=2*n*maxc/(s*s)
       if cur_score < best_score*0.999: best... (maybe p improved externally)
       w=_smooth_weights(c,tau_factor)
       # gradient step
       h=_gradient_vector(p,w)
       h = h - h.mean()
       norm=np.sqrt(np.mean(h*h))
       if norm<1e-12: break
       for mult in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.125]:
           logp=np.log(p+1e-300) - mult*(h/norm)
           p2=np.exp(logp-np.max(logp)); p2/=p2.sum()
           c2=_convolution_fft(p2); max2=c2.max(); s2=p2.sum()
           score2=2*n*max2/(s2*s2)
           if score2 < best_score:
               best=p2; best_score=score2; p=p2; eta=min(2.0, mult*1.3); break
       else:
           # random kick
           p=_noise_perturb(best, rng, sigma=0.02)
           eta=0.1
       it +=1
       if it%100==0: tau_factor=min(200, tau_factor*1.1); print progress
    return best,best_score
```

Let's implement `_optimize_mirror` with a bit of stochasticity: occasionally add small random to log p before step. Use global rng.

Pseudo:
```
def _mirror_optimize(p, deadline, tag, rng):
    p = np.asarray(p,dtype=float).copy(); p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); p/=p.sum()
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_fast(p)
    n=p.size
    tau_factor=10.0
    eta=0.25
    it=0
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        if no_improve > 200:
            # restart from best with random kick
            p=best.copy()
            kick=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n))
            p=p*kick; p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); p/=p.sum()
            no_improve=0
            eta=0.1
        c=_convolution_fft(p)
        maxc=float(c.max())
        if maxc<=0: break
        # current score
        s=float(p.sum())
        cur=2*n*maxc/(s*s)
        if cur < best_score*(1-1e-7):
            best=p.copy(); best_score=cur
        w=_smooth_weights(c,tau_factor)
        h=_gradient_vector(p,w)
        h -= np.mean(h)
        norm=np.sqrt(np.mean(h*h)) + 1e-300
        h = h / norm
        # maybe scale by 1/n? Already normalized.
        accepted=False
        for mult in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta, 0.125*eta, 0.0625*eta):
            logp=np.log(p) - mult*h
            # add tiny noise to avoid exact
            if it % 50 == 0:
                logp += rng.normal(0,0.002,n)
            p2=np.exp(logp-np.max(logp)); p2=np.maximum(p2,1e-300); p2/=p2.sum()
            c2=_convolution_fft(p2); max2=float(c2.max()); s2=float(p2.sum())
            score2=2*n*max2/(s2*s2)
            if score2 < best_score:
                best=p2.copy(); best_score=score2; p=p2; eta=min(2.0, mult*1.25); accepted=True; break
        if not accepted:
            eta *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1
            if eta < 1e-3:
                # try random perturb from best
                p=best.copy()
                noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,n))
                p=p*noise; p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); p/=p.sum()
                eta=0.1; no_improve=0
        it += 1
        if it % 200 == 0:
            tau_factor = min(100.0, tau_factor*1.05)
            print(f"{tag} it={it} best={best_score:.6f}")
    return best,best_score
```
If score differences are tiny, `score2 < best_score` may be too strict; include tolerance: `score2 <= best_score * (1 - 1e-10)` or `score2 < best_score`. Use current best not current p? If p is worse, trying to beat best may be hard. Use compare to p's current score maybe to move downhill; but to keep global, only update best if lower. For p movement, if score2 < cur and score2 < some? If p worse, we might need climb. Simpler: always move to score2 if score2 < cur (downhill) even if >best; and update best if lower. Param search implementation details:
```
def _param_search(n, deadline):
    base=_central_sequence(n)
    best=base.copy(); best_score=_score_fast(base)
    base_norm=base/base.sum()
    # normalize doesn't affect score, but for p maybe
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    modes=[]
    modes.append(('lin', x-0.5))
    modes.append(('quad', 4*x*(1-x)-0.5))
    for k in [1,2,3,4]:
        for ph in [0, np.pi/2, np.pi, 3*np.pi/2]:
            modes.append((f'cos{k}{ph}', np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x+ph)))
    alphas=np.linspace(-0.9,0.9,19)
    for name, phi in modes:
        # maybe zero mean phi? Not necessary but can center: phi = phi - mean(phi*base?)? For shape, center to avoid pure scale? Since scale invariant, constant offset irrelevant. We can subtract weighted mean to keep sum shape similar: phi = phi - np.mean(phi*base)/np.mean(base)? No need.
        for alpha in alphas:
            mult=1.0+alpha*phi
            if np.any(mult<=0): continue
            a=base*mult
            sc=_score_fast(a)
            if sc<best_score:
                best=a.copy(); best_score=sc
                print
        if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
    # exponential ramps
    for beta in np.linspace(-2,2,9):
        mult=np.exp(beta*x)
        a=base*mult
        sc=_score_fast(a)
        update
    return best,best_score
```

Local perturbation on best sequence (raw, not normalized) with multiplicative blocks:
```
def _local_search(a, deadline, rng):
    p=a/a.sum() if a.sum()>0 else a
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_fast(p)
    n=p.size
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       q=p.copy()
       # choose 1-3 blocks
       for _ in range(rng.integers(1,4)):
          width=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//200), max(3,n//10)))
          start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
          q[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,width))
       if rng.random()<0.2:
          q = 0.5*(q+q[::-1])
       q=np.maximum(q,1e-300); q/=q.sum()
       sc=_score_fast(q)
       if sc < best_score:
           p=q; best=q; best_score=sc; print
       it+=1
       if it%500==0: print
    return best,best_score
```
May not find big improvements but polish.

Coordinate transfer:
```
def _transfer_search(p, deadline, rng, n_pairs_steps):
   n=len(p); best=p; best_score=score
   while time:
      i=int(rng.integers(0,n//2)); j=int(rng.integers(n//2,n))
      # choose delta fraction of p_i
      frac=float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.2))
      q=p.copy()
      amt=q[i]*frac
      q[i]-=amt; q[j]+=amt
      q=np.maximum(q,0); q/=q.sum()
      sc=score(q)
      if sc<best_score: update
```

Code for `_smooth_weights`:
```
def _smooth_weights(c, tau_factor):
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc<=0:
        return np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64)/c.size
    tau=tau_factor / maxc
    vals=tau*(c - maxc)
    vals=np.clip(vals, -700.0, 700.0)
    ex=np.exp(vals)
    s=float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
        return np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64)/c.size
    w=ex/s
    return w
```
Since vals max0, sum <=m, no overflow.

In `_mirror_optimize`, if `tau_factor` too high, weights may underflow to only max point. For central all active equal => exp(0)=1 all active; good. For general, sharp.

`_score_fast` uses `_convolution_fft` which clips negative; if p has extremely small values, max maybe 0? no.

```
def _eval_list(seq):
    try:
        val=float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        val=np.inf
    return val
```

Implement `_linear_conv(x,y)`:
```
def _linear_conv(x,y):
    need=len(x)+len(y)-1
    nfft=_next_pow2(need)
    X=np.fft.fft(x, nfft)
    Y=np.fft.fft(y, nfft)
    out=np.fft.ifft(X*Y).real[:need]
    out[out<0]=0.0
    return out
```
This is used for A length n and B length 2n-1. `np.fft.fft` complex; OK.

For score `_autocorr_fft(a)`:
Could use `_linear_conv(a,a)` but uses FFT complex. Or use rfft for speed:
```
def _convolution_fft(a):
   n=len(a); need=2*n-1; nfft=_next_pow2(need)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
   ac=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fa),nfft)[:need]
   ac[ac<0]=0
   return ac
```
Good.

Random kick function:

```
def _kick(p, rng, sigma):
    q=p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,p.size))
    q=np.maximum(q,1e-300); q/=q.sum(); return q
```

```
def _random_start(n,rng, base):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
```

```
def _exp_prefix_search(n, deadline):
    c = _central_sequence(n)
    total = float(c.sum())
    bases = (c, c[::-1])
    best_a = c.copy(); best_score = _score_fast(c)
    best_base_idx=0; best_flip=True; best_beta=0.0
    betas = np.linspace(0.0, 25.0, 201)
    for bi, base in enumerate(bases):
        cum = np.cumsum(base) / total
        for flip in (True, False):
            A = cum[::-1] if flip else cum
            for beta in betas:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline: return best_a,best_score,...? But need return only seq/score.
                a = base * np.exp(-beta * A)
                sc = _score_fast(a)
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy(); best_base_idx=bi; best_flip=flip; best_beta=float(beta)
    # refine if time left
    if best_beta > 0.1:
       step=(betas[1]-betas[0])
       local_betas=np.linspace(max(0,best_beta-10*step), best_beta+10*step,101)
       base=bases[best_base_idx]; A=...; evaluate
    return best_a,best_score
```

Let's produce final fenced code. Ensure no lambdas. All helpers top-level. Use `print` but not too spammy. In `_mirror_optimize`, printing every 2000 iterations maybe to avoid flood. Use iteration count; if >0. In `_exp_prefix_search`, print best per n. OK.

Let's write final.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for low-score non-negative step-function heights.

The score is
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

This program combines several ideas:
1. The central-binomial coefficients C_i = binom(2i,i)/4^i give a very good
   one-sided "singular" construction whose first n autocorrelation/convolution
   entries are exactly 1.
2. A strong one-parameter deformation
       a_i = C_i * exp(-beta * prefix_fraction[n-1-i])
   is searched.  It decreases the early entries and relaxes the bound in a
   way that often lowers the normalized peak significantly.
3. A mirror-descent optimizer in probability space minimizes a smooth maximum
   of the convolution, starting from the best construction found so far.
4. Lightweight local multiplicative perturbation search is used to polish.

The final answer is normalized to sum 1 before being returned.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _central_sequence(n):
    """Positive sequence C_i = binom(2i,i)/4^i."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        v *= (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
        c[i] = v
    return c


def _convolution_fft(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a, using FFT and clipping numerical negatives."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = _next_pow2(need)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa.conj(), nfft)[:need]
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out


def _linear_conv(x, y):
    """Linear convolution x*y, allowing different lengths."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    need = x.size + y.size - 1
    nfft = _next_pow2(need)
    X = np.fft.fft(x, nfft)
    Y = np.fft.fft(y, nfft)
    out = np.fft.ifft(X * Y).real[:need]
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out


def _score_fast(a):
    """Fast surrogate for evaluate_sequence, using FFT convolution."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    c = _convolution_fft(a)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mc / (s * s))


def _smooth_softmax_weights(c, tau_factor):
    """Smooth max weights for convolution entries c."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    tau = float(tau_factor) / maxc
    vals = tau * (c - maxc)
    vals = np.clip(vals, -700.0, 700.0)
    ex = np.exp(vals)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    w = ex / s
    return w


def _gradient_vector(p, w):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k conv(p,p)_k with respect to p, on the simplex.
    Returns a vector of length len(p).
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    # A has nonnegative exponents; p_j is placed at exponent n-1-j.
    A = p[::-1].copy()
    B = w.copy()
    conv = _linear_conv(A, B)
    # q_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}
    q = conv[(n - 1):(2 * n - 1)]
    return 2.0 * q


def _normalize_simplex(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        n = p.size
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return p / s


def _random_kick(p, rng, sigma):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    q = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), p.size))
    return _normalize_simplex(q)


def _prefix_exponential_search(n, deadline):
    """
    Search a_i = base * exp(-beta * A_i) on central-binomial bases.
    A_i is a cumulative fraction from one side or the other.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0 or time.monotonic() > deadline:
        return None, np.inf

    c = _central_sequence(n)
    total = float(c.sum())
    if total <= 0.0:
        return None, np.inf

    bases = (c, c[::-1])
    best_a = c.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(c)
    best_base_idx = 0
    best_flip = True
    best_beta = 0.0

    betas = np.linspace(0.0, 25.0, 201, dtype=np.float64)

    for bi, base in enumerate(bases):
        cum = np.cumsum(base) / total
        for flip in (True, False):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                return best_a, best_score
            A = cum[::-1] if flip else cum
            for beta in betas:
                a = base * np.exp(-float(beta) * A)
                sc = _score_fast(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    best_base_idx = bi
                    best_flip = flip
                    best_beta = float(beta)

    # Local refinement around the best discovered beta.
    base = bases[best_base_idx]
    cum = np.cumsum(base) / total
    A = cum[::-1] if best_flip else cum
    if best_beta > 0.0:
        step = float(betas[1] - betas[0]) if betas.size > 1 else 0.1
        lo = max(0.0, best_beta - 20.0 * step)
        hi = best_beta + 20.0 * step
        refined = np.linspace(lo, hi, 161, dtype=np.float64)
        for beta in refined:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            a = base * np.exp(-float(beta) * A)
            sc = _score_fast(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def _param_search_simple(n, deadline):
    """A few additional low-dimensional multiplier searches on central base."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0 or time.monotonic() > deadline:
        return None, np.inf

    base = _central_sequence(n)
    best_a = base.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(base)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    modes = []
    modes.append((x - 0.5,))
    modes.append((4.0 * x * (1.0 - x) - 0.5,))
    for k in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        for ph in (0.0, 0.7853981633974483, 1.5707963267948966, 2.356194490192345, 3.141592653589793):
            modes.append((np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph),))

    alphas = np.linspace(-0.9, 0.9, 25, dtype=np.float64)

    for phi, in modes:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        for alpha in alphas:
            mult = 1.0 + float(alpha) * phi
            if np.any(mult <= 0.0):
                continue
            a = base * mult
            sc = _score_fast(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()

    # exponential ramps
    for beta in np.linspace(-2.5, 2.5, 21, dtype=np.float64):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        a = base * np.exp(float(beta) * x)
        sc = _score_fast(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def _mirror_optimize(p, deadline, tag, rng):
    """
    Mirror descent on the simplex minimizing a smooth version of:
        max_k conv(p,p)_k
    Since sum(p)=1, this is equivalent to minimizing the score up to 2n.
    """
    p = _normalize_simplex(p)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return p, np.inf

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(p)
    current_score = best_score

    tau_factor = 15.0
    eta = 0.18
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _convolution_fft(p)
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
            break
        s = float(np.sum(p))
        if s <= 0.0:
            break
        current_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc / (s * s))
        if current_score < best_score:
            best_score = current_score
            best_p = p.copy()

        w = _smooth_softmax_weights(c, tau_factor)
        h = _gradient_vector(p, w)
        h -= float(np.mean(h))
        norm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(h * h)))
        if not np.isfinite(norm) or norm <= 1e-300:
            break
        h = h / norm

        accepted = False
        for mult in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta, 0.125 * eta):
            logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)) - float(mult) * h
            if it % 128 == 0:
                logp += rng.normal(0.0, 0.0015, n)
            p2 = np.exp(logp - np.max(logp))
            p2 = np.maximum(p2, 1e-300)
            p2 = p2 / float(np.sum(p2))

            c2 = _convolution_fft(p2)
            max2 = float(np.max(c2))
            s2 = float(np.sum(p2))
            if not np.isfinite(max2) or max2 <= 0.0 or s2 <= 0.0:
                continue
            score2 = float(2.0 * n * max2 / (s2 * s2))
            if np.isfinite(score2) and score2 < current_score - 1e-12:
                p = p2
                current_score = score2
                if score2 < best_score:
                    best_score = score2
                    best_p = p2.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                accepted = True
                eta = min(2.5, float(mult) * 1.12)
                break

        if not accepted:
            eta *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1
            if eta < 1e-3:
                # Restart from best with a modest random kick.
                p = _random_kick(best_p, rng, 0.07)
                eta = 0.12
                no_improve = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 512 == 0:
            tau_factor = min(250.0, tau_factor * 1.04)
            print(f"[{tag}] it={it} best={best_score:.8f} current={current_score:.8f} tau={tau_factor:.2f}")

    return best_p, best_score


def _local_multiplicative_search(a, deadline, rng):
    """Polish a best sequence by random positive block perturbations."""
    p = _normalize_simplex(a)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return p, np.inf

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(p)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        q = p.copy()
        blocks = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        for _ in range(blocks):
            width_min = max(2, n // 500)
            width_max = max(width_min + 1, n // 10)
            width = int(rng.integers(width_min, width_max + 1))
            if width >= n:
                width = max(1, n // 4)
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            stop = min(n, start + width)
            q[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.035, stop - start))

        if rng.random() < 0.15:
            q = 0.5 * (q + q[::-1])

        q = _normalize_simplex(q)
        sc = _score_fast(q)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_p = q.copy()
            best_score = sc
            p = q

        it += 1
        if it % 4096 == 0:
            print(f"[local] it={it} best={best_score:.8f}")

    return best_p, best_score


def _try_previous_sequence():
    """If the externally supplied height_sequence_1 is available, return it."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.isfinite(arr).all():
        return None
    if float(np.sum(arr)) <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    """
    Generate a sequence minimizing the provided evaluation function.
    Returns a Python list of non-negative floats.
    """
    try:
        budget_s = float(kwargs.get("budget_s", budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget_s):
        budget_s = 2400.0
    budget_s = max(30.0, min(float(budget_s), 2400.0))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if np.isfinite(seed) else 42)
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(5.0, budget_s - 18.0)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf
    best_by_n = {}

    def update_best(a, score, tag):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
            return
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if not np.isfinite(a).all() or float(np.sum(a)) <= 0.0:
            return
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            score = _score_fast(a)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_seq = a.copy()
            best_score = score
            n = int(a.size)
            if n not in best_by_n:
                best_by_n[n] = a.copy()
            else:
                old = best_by_n[n]
                old_score = _score_fast(old)
                if np.isfinite(score) and (not np.isfinite(old_score) or score < old_score):
                    best_by_n[n] = a.copy()
            print(f"[update] {tag}: score={score:.8f} n={a.size}")

    # Initial previous sequence, if any.
    prev = _try_previous_sequence()
    if prev is not None:
        sc = _score_fast(prev)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            update_best(prev, sc, "previous")

    # Search lengths.
    lengths = [128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096]

    # Phase 1: good deterministic candidates.
    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c = _central_sequence(n)
        sc = _score_fast(c)
        update_best(c, sc, f"central n={n}")

        dl2 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
        pfx, psc = _prefix_exponential_search(n, dl2)
        if pfx is not None and np.isfinite(psc):
            update_best(pfx, psc, f"prefix-exp n={n}")

        # A small extra low-dimensional search.
        dl3 = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 18.0)
        par, ps = _param_search_simple(n, dl3)
        if par is not None and np.isfinite(ps):
            update_best(par, ps, f"param n={n}")

        if n not in best_by_n:
            best_by_n[n] = c.copy()

    # If nothing worked, fall back to a safe sequence.
    if best_seq is None:
        fallback = _central_sequence(1024)
        best_seq = fallback
        best_score = _score_fast(fallback)
        best_by_n[1024] = fallback.copy()

    # Phase 2: mirror descent from strong starts.
    # Prefer longer sequences; allocate time dynamically.
    mirror_specs = [
        (4096, 330.0),
        (2048, 300.0),
        (1024, 220.0),
        (512, 120.0),
        (256, 70.0),
        (128, 30.0),
    ]
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    total_req = sum(w for _, w in mirror_specs)
    reserve_local = min(350.0, 0.22 * remaining)
    mirror_budget = max(0.0, remaining - reserve_local)
    scale = mirror_budget / total_req if total_req > 0 else 0.0

    for n, base_time in mirror_specs:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 30.0:
            break
        t = max(0.0, base_time * scale)
        if t < 35.0:
            continue
        end = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + t)

        start_a = best_by_n.get(n, _central_sequence(n))
        start_p = _normalize_simplex(start_a)

        # Also try a random smooth kick from the best start for diversity.
        kick_p = _random_kick(start_p, rng, 0.04)

        p1, s1 = _mirror_optimize(start_p, end, f"mirror n={n}", rng)
        if p1 is not None and np.isfinite(s1):
            update_best(p1, s1, f"mirror n={n}")

        if time.monotonic() < end - 2.0:
            end2 = min(end, time.monotonic() + max(5.0, t * 0.25))
            p2, s2 = _mirror_optimize(kick_p, end2, f"mirror-kick n={n}", rng)
            if p2 is not None and np.isfinite(s2):
                update_best(p2, s2, f"mirror-kick n={n}")

    # Phase 3: local polish on global best.
    local_deadline = min(deadline - 8.0, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, reserve_local * 0.6))
    if time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
        local_deadline = min(deadline - 8.0, local_deadline)
        lp, lsc = _local_multiplicative_search(best_seq, local_deadline, rng)
        if lp is not None and np.isfinite(lsc):
            update_best(lp, lsc, "local-polish")

    # Final validation and selection among a few top candidates.
    final_candidates = []
    if best_seq is not None:
        final_candidates.append(best_seq.copy())

    for n in (4096, 2048, 1024, 512, 256, 128):
        if n in best_by_n:
            final_candidates.append(best_by_n[n].copy())

    # Rank candidates by fast score to evaluate only the best few.
    ranked = []
    for arr in final_candidates:
        sc = _score_fast(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            ranked.append((sc, arr))
    ranked.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])

    chosen = None
    chosen_score = np.inf

    # Evaluate the best few with the authoritative evaluator.
    for _, arr in ranked[:8]:
        q = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
        q = np.clip(q, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(np.sum(q))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            continue
        q = q / s
        q = np.clip(q, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if q.size == 0:
            continue
        try:
            exact = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in q]))
        except Exception:
            exact = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < chosen_score:
            chosen_score = exact
            chosen = q

    # Fallback if validation failed for all.
    if chosen is None:
        fallback = best_seq if best_seq is not None else _central_sequence(1024)
        q = np.asarray(fallback, dtype=np.float64)
        q = np.clip(q, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(np.sum(q))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            q = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            q = q / s
        chosen = q

    return [float(x) for x in chosen]
```
